\"/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/__pycache__/dqn_de_util.cpython-312.pyc\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/__pycache__/report.cpython-312.pyc\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/__pycache__/utils.cpython-312.pyc\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dataset.csv\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_de_util.py\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/report.py\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/utils.py\n---\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\nimport csv\nimport math\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\nfrom config import set_global_seed, SEED, MODEL_NAME\n\n# Set global seed at module load time\nset_global_seed()\n\n\ndef load_opt_vals():\n    \"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'dataset.csv')\n    opt_vals = {}\n    if os.path.exists(dataset_path):\n        with open(dataset_path, 'r') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    \n    Args:\n        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    \n    Returns:\n        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    import cocoex\n\n    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    \n    # Create or use provided controller\n    if controller is None:\n        # load meta learning implementations\n        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and          opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE     spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u89e3\u6790\n    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n\n    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n    return {\n 70\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    71\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    72\t\n    73\t    - Early iterations: high F self.actions = []\n   171\t        for fi in self.F_vals:\n   172\t            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n   173\t                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n   174\t seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n   178\t\n   179\t        # Training setup\n   180\t        self.training = training\n   181\t        self.update_freq = update_freq\n   182\t        self.batch_size = batch_size\n   183\t        self.gamma = gamma\n   184\t        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n   185\t        self.step_count = 0\n   186\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   187\t epsilon_decay\n   197\t        if training:\n   198\t            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n   199\t            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n   200\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   201\t            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n   202\t            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n   203\t            self.replay_buffer = []\n   204\t            self.buffer_pos = 0\n   205\t\n   206\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n   207\t            try:\n   208\t                self.load(model_path)\n   209\t            except Exception:\n   210\t                pass\n   211\t\n   212\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n   213\t        return None\n   214\t\n   215\t    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n   216\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n   217\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n   218\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n   219\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n   220\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n   221\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n   222\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n   223\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n   224\t        else:\n   225\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n   226\t        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range           idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t       if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n        self.mu_F = float(self.F)\n        self.mu_CR = float(self.CR)\n        self.archive = []\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done =           if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # Generate trial vectors\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\n                else:\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n                \n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\n                \n                # Mutation and crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\n                \n                # Boundary handling: clip\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\n            \n            # Evaluate trial vectors\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n This is\nthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\n\"\"\"\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nimport os\ntry:\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    # Set global seed at module load time\n    set_global_seed()\nexcept Exception:\n    # Fallback if config module is not available\n    SEED = 42\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n        pass\n\n\nclass ParamController(ABC):\n    @abstractmethod\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n        self.params = dict(params)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        return dict(self.params)\n\n\nclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n        self.w_start = w_start\n        self.w_end = w_end\n        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n\n\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n        self.f_start = f_start\n        self.f_end = f_end\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n        self.last_gbest = None\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n            strategy = 'rand/1'\n        else:\n            strategy = 'jade'\n\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\"\n    }\n    </json>",

t) + self.w_end * t\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n\n\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n        self.f_start = f_start\n        self.f_end = f_end\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        self.it = 0\n        self.last_gbest = None\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n            strategy = 'rand/1'\n        else:\n            strategy = 'jade'\n\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n\n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\"\n    }\n    </json>"

If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"sed -n '80,260p' /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            # Use controller-provided values as the base for adaptive means\n            self.mu_F = F\n            self.mu_CR = CR\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # JADE-style strategy: pbest mutation + external archive + per-individual adaptive F/CR\n            p = 0.1  # proportion of top solutions for pbest\n            n_pbest = max(1, int(pop_size * p))\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness, kind='stable')[:n_pbest]\n            pbest_pool = pop[sorted_idx]\n            \n            # Combined pool of current population and archive for the second difference vector\n            archive_pool = np.asarray(self.archive, dtype=float) if len(self.archive) > 0 else np.empty((0, self.dim))\n            combined_pool = np.vstack([pop, archive_pool]) if len(archive_pool) > 0 else pop\n            combined_size = len(combined_pool)\n            \n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            \n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR from means\n                F_i = float(np.clip(self.mu_F + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\n                    F_i = 0.1\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(self.mu_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                F_vals[i] = F_i\n                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\n                \n                # Pick pbest from top p% individuals\n                pb = pbest_pool[int(rng.randint(0, n_pbest))]\n                \n                # Pick r1 from population, excluding i\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) > 0:\n                    r1 = pop[int(rng.choice(candidates))]\n                else:\n                    r1 = pop[int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))]\n                \n                # Pick r2 from combined population+archive, preferably not r1/i\n                attempts = 0\n                while attempts < 10:\n                    r2 = combined_pool[int(rng.randint(0, combined_size))]\n                    if not np.array_equal(r2, r1) and not np.array_equal(r2, pop[i]):\n                        break\n                    attempts += 1\n                \n                # Mutant: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pb - pop[i]) + F_i * (r1 - r2)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover with at least one dimension from mutant\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        new_pop[i, j] = mutant[j]\n                \n                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization for out-of-bounds dimensions\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if new_pop[i, j] < lb[j] or new_pop[i, j] > ub[j]:\n                        new_pop[i, j] = lb[j] + rng.random() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n            \n            # Evaluate trial vectors\n            SF = []\n            SCR = []\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with archive update\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    SF.append(F_vals[i])\n                    SCR.append(CR_vals[i])\n                    # Add the replaced parent to the external archive\n                    if len(self.archive) < pop_size:\n                        self.archive.append(pop[i].copy())\n                    elif len(self.archive) > 0:\n                        self.archive[int(rng.randint(0, len(self.archive)))] = pop[i].copy()\n                    \n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n            \n            # Update parameter means based on this generation's successful F/CR values\n            if SF:\n                c = 0.1\n                self.mu_F = (1.0 - c) * self.mu_F + c * float(np.mean(SF))\n                self.mu_CR = (1.0 - c) * self.mu_CR + c * float(np.mean(SCR))\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            # Use controller-provided values as the base for adaptive means\n            self.mu_F = F\n            self.mu_CR = CR\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # JADE-style strategy: pbest mutation + external archive + per-individual adaptive F/CR\n            p = 0.1  # proportion of top solutions for pbest\n            n_pbest = max(1, int(pop_size * p))\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness, kind='stable')[:n_pbest]\n            pbest_pool = pop[sorted_idx]\n            \n            # Combined pool of current population and archive for the second difference vector\n            archive_pool = np.asarray(self.archive, dtype=float) if len(self.archive) > 0 else np.empty((0, self.dim))\n            combined_pool = np.vstack([pop, archive_pool]) if len(archive_pool) > 0 else pop\n            combined_size = len(combined_pool)\n            \n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            \n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR from means\n                F_i = float(np.clip(self.mu_F + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\n                    F_i = 0.1\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(self.mu_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                F_vals[i] = F_i\n                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\n                \n                # Pick pbest from top p% individuals\n                pb = pbest_pool[int(rng.randint(0, n_pbest))]\n                \n                # Pick r1 from population, excluding i\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) > 0:\n                    r1 = pop[int(rng.choice(candidates))]\n                else:\n                    r1 = pop[int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))]\n                \n                # Pick r2 from combined population+archive, preferably not r1/i\n                attempts = 0\n                while attempts < 10:\n                    r2 = combined_pool[int(rng.randint(0, combined_size))]\n                    if not np.array_equal(r2, r1) and not np.array_equal(r2, pop[i]):\n                        break\n                    attempts += 1\n                \n                # Mutant: x_i + F*(pbest - x_i) + F*(r1 - r2)\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pb - pop[i]) + F_i * (r1 - r2)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover with at least one dimension from mutant\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        new_pop[i, j] = mutant[j]\n                \n                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization for out-of-bounds dimensions\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if new_pop[i, j] < lb[j] or new_pop[i, j] > ub[j]:\n                        new_pop[i, j] = lb[j] + rng.random() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n            \n            # Evaluate trial vectors\n            SF = []\n            SCR = []\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with archive update\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    SF.append(F_vals[i])\n                    SCR.append(CR_vals[i])\n                    # Add the replaced parent to the external archive\n                    if len(self.archive) < pop_size:\n                        self.archive.append(pop[i].copy())\n                    elif len(self.archive) > 0:\n                        self.archive[int(rng.randint(0, len(self.archive)))] = pop[i].copy()\n                    \n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n            \n            # Update parameter means based on this generation's successful F/CR values\n            if SF:\n                c = 0.1\n                self.mu_F = (1.0 - c) * self.mu_F + c * float(np.mean(SF))\n                self.mu_CR = (1.0 - c) * self.mu_CR + c * float(np.mean(SCR))\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>"
